逐字符扫描：遇到 {{ 找到 }}，中间原样放进 (?:...)；遇到 [[：带 : 是捕获，变成命名组 (?P<name>...)（名字里的 $ 要换成合法字符），否则用 re.escape(env[name])；空白序列 → \s+；其他字符 re.escape。

L2NEXT 和 SAME 用“行边界”拆解与常见坑（中度剧透）

写一个 _line_bounds(text, pos) 返回 pos 所在行的 [start, end)。SAME：在上一个匹配的结尾到行尾之间搜。NEXT：取上一个匹配所在行的下一行的边界，在那一行里搜。

L3NOT 的区间几乎是答案（重度剧透）

遇到 NOT 先挂起；下一个正向匹配（或 DAG 组的最早匹配）确定后，检查 [上一个匹配的结尾, 这个匹配的开头)；块结束时，剩下的 NOT 检查到块尾。LABEL：先按顺序找到所有 LABEL 的位置，再对每个块单独跑。

参考解

解solutions/t5_filecheck.py先自己写完、跑过测试再看
solutions/t5_filecheck.py193 行
"""T5 · A mini FileCheck: how Triton's lit tests decide pass / fail — reference solution.

Every file under triton/test/ starts with a RUN line such as
    // RUN: triton-opt %s -split-input-file -tritongpu-coalesce | FileCheck %s
lit runs the pass, pipes the output to LLVM's FileCheck, and FileCheck matches the `// CHECK...`
comments of the same file against that output. Supported here (the subset Triton tests use most):

  CHECK:        match somewhere at or after the current position
  CHECK-NEXT:   match on the line right after the previous match
  CHECK-SAME:   match on the same line as the previous match, after it
  CHECK-NOT:    must not appear between the previous match and the next positive match
  CHECK-LABEL:  splits input into blocks first; checks never cross a label
  CHECK-DAG:    a group of consecutive DAG checks may match in any order
  CHECK-COUNT-n: match n times in a row (each after the previous)

Patterns: literal text, except {{regex}} and [[VAR:regex]] (capture) / [[VAR]] (use), with
the `$` prefix ([[$VAR]]) meaning "global": with --enable-var-scope only those survive a CHECK-LABEL. Whitespace runs in the pattern
match any whitespace run in the input (FileCheck's default canonicalization).
"""
import re

DIRECTIVE = re.compile(r"(?://|;|#)\s*(CHECK(?:-NEXT|-SAME|-NOT|-LABEL|-DAG|-COUNT-\d+)?):(.*)$")


class CheckFailure(AssertionError):
    pass


def parse_checks(source, prefix="CHECK"):
    """[(kind, pattern, line_no)] from a test file. kind: 'CHECK', 'NEXT', 'SAME', 'NOT', 'LABEL', 'DAG', 'COUNT'."""
    out = []
    rx = re.compile(DIRECTIVE.pattern.replace("CHECK", re.escape(prefix)))
    for i, line in enumerate(source.splitlines(), 1):
        m = rx.search(line)
        if not m:
            continue
        tag, pat = m.group(1), m.group(2).strip()
        kind = tag[len(prefix):].lstrip("-") or "CHECK"
        if kind.startswith("COUNT-"):
            out.extend([("CHECK", pat, i)] * int(kind[6:]))
        else:
            out.append((kind, pat, i))
    return out


def compile_pattern(pat, env):
    """Turn a FileCheck pattern into a Python regex. Returns (regex, [capture variable names])."""
    rx, names, i = "", [], 0
    while i < len(pat):
        if pat.startswith("{{", i):
            j = pat.index("}}", i + 2)
            rx += "(?:" + pat[i + 2:j] + ")"
            i = j + 2
        elif pat.startswith("[[", i):
            j = pat.index("]]", i + 2)
            body = pat[i + 2:j]
            if ":" in body:
                name, sub = body.split(":", 1)
                key = re.sub(r"\W", "_", name)
                rx += f"(?P<{key}>{sub})"
                names.append(name)
            else:
                if body not in env:
                    raise CheckFailure(f"undefined variable [[{body}]]")
                rx += re.escape(env[body])
            i = j + 2
        elif pat[i].isspace():
            while i < len(pat) and pat[i].isspace():
                i += 1
            rx += r"\s+"
        else:
            rx += re.escape(pat[i])
            i += 1
    return re.compile(rx), names


def _search(pat, text, pos, end, env):
    rx, names = compile_pattern(pat, env)
    m = rx.search(text, pos, end)
    if m:
        for n in names:
            env[n] = m.group(re.sub(r"\W", "_", n))
    return m


def _line_bounds(text, pos):
    s = text.rfind("\n", 0, pos) + 1
    e = text.find("\n", pos)
    return s, (len(text) if e < 0 else e)


def _check_block(checks, text, start, end, env):
    pos = start
    last = None             # (match_start, match_end) of the previous positive match
    pending_not = []
    i = 0
    while i < len(checks):
        kind, pat, ln = checks[i]
        if kind == "NOT":
            pending_not.append((pat, ln))
            i += 1
            continue
        if kind == "DAG":
            group = []
            while i < len(checks) and checks[i][0] == "DAG":
                group.append(checks[i])
                i += 1
            used, hi = [], pos
            for _, p, l in group:
                rx, names = compile_pattern(p, env)
                found = None
                for m in rx.finditer(text, pos, end):
                    if all(m.end() <= a or m.start() >= b for a, b in used):
                        found = m
                        break
                if not found:
                    raise CheckFailure(f"line {l}: CHECK-DAG: {p!r} not found")
                for n in names:
                    env[n] = found.group(re.sub(r"\W", "_", n))
                used.append((found.start(), found.end()))
                hi = max(hi, found.end())
            _check_nots(pending_not, text, pos, min(a for a, _ in used), env)
            pending_not, pos, last = [], hi, (min(a for a, _ in used), hi)
            continue
        if kind == "SAME":
            if last is None:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-SAME with no previous match")
            _, le = _line_bounds(text, last[1] - 1 if last[1] > last[0] else last[1])
            m = _search(pat, text, last[1], le, env)
            if not m:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-SAME: {pat!r} not on the same line")
        elif kind == "NEXT":
            if last is None:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-NEXT with no previous match")
            _, le = _line_bounds(text, max(last[1] - 1, last[0]))
            if le >= end:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-NEXT: no next line")
            ns, ne = _line_bounds(text, le + 1)
            m = _search(pat, text, ns, ne, env)
            if not m:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-NEXT: {pat!r} is not on the next line")
        else:
            m = _search(pat, text, pos, end, env)
            if not m:
                raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK: {pat!r} not found")
        _check_nots(pending_not, text, pos, m.start(), env)
        pending_not = []
        last, pos = (m.start(), m.end()), m.end()
        i += 1
    _check_nots(pending_not, text, pos, end, env)


def _check_nots(nots, text, lo, hi, env):
    for pat, ln in nots:
        rx, _ = compile_pattern(pat, env)
        m = rx.search(text, lo, hi)
        if m:
            raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-NOT: {pat!r} found: {m.group(0)!r}")


def filecheck(check_source, output, prefix="CHECK", enable_var_scope=False):
    """Raise CheckFailure (an AssertionError) if `output` does not satisfy the CHECKs in check_source."""
    checks = parse_checks(check_source, prefix)
    if not checks:
        raise CheckFailure("no check strings found")
    env = {}
    # 1. labels cut the input into blocks
    labels = [(i, c) for i, c in enumerate(checks) if c[0] == "LABEL"]
    if not labels:
        _check_block(checks, output, 0, len(output), env)
        return True
    starts = []
    pos = 0
    for _, (_, pat, ln) in labels:
        m = _search(pat, output, pos, len(output), env)
        if not m:
            raise CheckFailure(f"line {ln}: CHECK-LABEL: {pat!r} not found")
        starts.append((m.start(), m.end()))
        pos = m.end()
    idx = [i for i, _ in labels] + [len(checks)]
    if labels[0][0] > 0:
        _check_block(checks[:labels[0][0]], output, 0, starts[0][0], env)
    for b, (s, e) in enumerate(starts):
        block_end = starts[b + 1][0] if b + 1 < len(starts) else len(output)
        if enable_var_scope:     # FileCheck --enable-var-scope: only [[$VAR]] survives a label
            env = {k: v for k, v in env.items() if k.startswith("$")}
        _check_block(checks[idx[b] + 1: idx[b + 1]], output, e, block_end, env)
    return True


def split_input_file(text):
    """triton-opt -split-input-file: chunks separated by a line `// -----`."""
    return [c.strip("\n") for c in re.split(r"^// -----\s*$", text, flags=re.M)]

在 Triton 仓库里：读一个、改一个、写一个

  1. 读：打开 coalesce.mlir，对着 T2 的手算解释每一条 CHECK；用你的 filecheck() 跑一遍：filecheck(open("coalesce.mlir").read(), subprocess.check_output(["triton-opt", ...]))，和真 FileCheck 结果一致。
  2. 改：故意改坏 CoalesceUtils.cpp 里的 perThread 计算（比如去掉 128 / elemNumBits 的上限），重新 build，用 lit test/TritonGPU/coalesce.mlir -v 看哪条 CHECK 先失败、失败信息长什么样。
  3. 写：为 T4 找到的问题（或者 coalesce 的一个没被覆盖的情况，比如 tt.divisibility = 8）写一个新的 // ----- 分段测试：输入 IR + RUN 行 + 最少但足够的 CHECK。好的 CHECK 只检查你关心的东西，别把整个输出抄进去。
cd triton && pip install -e . --no-build-isolation      # 第一次 build
lit -v build/cmake.*/test/TritonGPU/coalesce.mlir        # 或：cd build/cmake.* && ninja check-triton-lit-tests
自测：为什么 Triton 的 CHECK 里到处是 `[[row_layout:#.*]]` 这种捕获，而不是直接写 `#blocked`？

layout alias 的名字（#blocked、#blocked1、#blocked2）是打印器按出现顺序分配的，一个无关的改动（多出一个 layout）就会让编号整体移位。捕获“那个内容是 sizePerThread = [1, 4], ... 的 alias”，后面再用 [[row_layout]] 引用，测试只依赖语义（layout 的内容和它被用在哪里），不依赖名字。这也是写新测试时最常被 reviewer 指出的问题之一。

Track L · Luminal（graph compiler）

primitive graph、e-graph 与 equality saturation、实测驱动的搜索、执行计划与 arena、Reference vs CUDA 对拍。

双主线 · Track L · Luminal（graph compiler） · 第 10 题

L1Graph semantics：把 relu(x @ w + b) 拆成 primitive ops

把 y = relu(x @ w + b) 交给 Luminal，它记录下来的不是三个“大算子”，而是十几个 primitive 节点：broadcast 视图、一个 [M, N, K] 的 Mul、一个 ReduceSum、一个 LessThan 和一个 Select。这道题用 Python 写一个 Luminal 式的“只记录、不计算”的图，给出节点语义表，再写一个按拓扑序执行的 reference runtime。

⏱ 40 min 难度 易 你写 mine/l1_graph.py验收 ./check.sh l1参考解 solutions/l1_graph.py 📖 单独讲解页
  1. 背景
  2. 源码与资料
  3. 要用到的知识
  4. 题目要求
  5. 题干
  6. 验收
  7. 提示
  8. 参考解
  9. 在 Luminal 里：看真正的图

背景

roadmap 的 L1：“构建 y = relu(x @ w + b)，标出 shape、dtype、broadcast 和每个 primitive。交付：graph DAG + 节点语义表。”

真实 Luminal 里这件事由 GraphTensor 的方法完成：

  • luminalmatmul：“broadcast + reduce in the operands' shared dtype, NOTHING else”——把两个操作数映射到 [..., M, N, K]，相乘，沿 K 求和；从不插入 cast。
  • luminalrelu = maximum_f32(0.)；luminalmaximum = lt(...).select(...)，外加一个 NaN 覆盖（注释解释了为什么不用 fmax）。
  • luminalexp = (x · log2(e)).exp2()：primitive 集里只有 Exp2。

源码与资料

luminalREADME：RISC-style architecture（15 个 primitive） · luminalsrc/logical/ir.rs：LogicalOp · luminalsrc/graph.rs · luminalfrontend/movement.rs：expand / permute

要用到的知识

规则 说明
broadcast 是显式的 二元 op 要求两边 shape 完全一致；b[N] 加到 [M, N] 上前要先 Expand
记录不计算 x + y 只往 graph.nodes 里追加一个节点，返回一个只含 id 的句柄
dtype 随节点走 LessThan 产出 bool；Select 的 dtype 跟随两个分支
拓扑序执行 reference runtime 从输出反向 DFS，得到“每个输入都在使用者之前”的顺序
类比 · 录音而不是现场演奏

PyTorch eager 像现场演奏：每写一行，乐手就弹一个音（x + y 立刻算出结果）。Luminal 像录音棚里先把整首曲子写成分轨乐谱（DAG），而且所有乐器都被拆成几种基本音色（primitive）。写完之后编曲（e-graph + 搜索）可以重新安排：把两条轨合并成一个和弦（fusion），用采样代替真乐手（cuBLASLt）。reference runtime 就是按乐谱老老实实一轨一轨弹一遍，用来核对编曲之后的版本有没有弹错（L5）。

题目要求

在 mine/l1_graph.py 里实现（Graph.add / tensor / constant、Tensor 的 movement 和二元 op、sum、exp2 已给出）：

函数 要点
Graph.topo_order() 从 outputs（没有就用最后一个节点）反向 DFS，后序
Graph.op_counts() 只数拓扑序里可达的节点
Graph.semantics_table() (id, op, inputs, shape, dtype, note)；Expand / Permute 的 note 里写上 view
Graph.materialized_bytes(elem_bytes) 非 view、非 Input / Constant 节点的 buffer 字节数之和
Tensor.matmul(w) self.expand(1, N)；w.permute([1, 0]).expand(0, M)；Mul；sum(2)
Tensor.relu() self.lt(zero).select(zero, self)
Tensor.exp() (self · log2(e)).exp2()
build_mlp(M, K, N) 返回 (g, x, w, b, y)
run(g, feeds) numpy 执行，返回 {node id: array}

题干

mine/l1_graph.py152 行
"""L1 · Graph semantics: y = relu(x @ w + b) as a Luminal-style primitive graph — your implementation (run ./check.sh l1).

Luminal records every tensor operation into a static DAG instead of running it (lazy, ahead of
time). High-level ops are *spelled* with a small primitive set (README: "Everything in Luminal
boils down to 15 primitive ops"; src/logical/ir.rs `LogicalOp` has the current full list):

  matmul(a, b)  = broadcast both to [M, N, K]  ->  Mul  ->  ReduceSum(K)     (src/frontend/matmul.rs)
  relu(x)       = maximum(x, 0) = Select(LessThan(x, 0), 0, x)                (src/frontend/unary.rs, binary.rs)
  exp(x)        = Exp2(x * log2(e))                                           (src/frontend/unary.rs)
  a + b[N]      = Add(a, Expand(b))  — broadcasting is a *view*, not a copy

This file is a ~150-line Python model of that design: a Graph of nodes with shapes and dtypes,
a GraphTensor API that records, a node-semantics table, and a reference runtime (numpy) that
executes the DAG in topological order — the analogue of Luminal's ReferenceRuntime.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

PRIMITIVES = {"Input", "Constant", "Add", "Mul", "Recip", "Exp2", "Log2", "Sqrt", "Sin", "LessThan",
              "Select", "ReduceSum", "ReduceMax", "Expand", "Permute", "Cast"}
MOVEMENT = {"Expand", "Permute"}          # views: change indexing, move no data


@dataclass
class Node:
    id: int
    op: str
    inputs: list
    shape: tuple
    dtype: str = "f32"
    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = []
        self.outputs = []

    def add(self, op, inputs, shape, dtype="f32", **attrs):
        assert op in PRIMITIVES, op
        n = Node(len(self.nodes), op, [t.id for t in inputs], tuple(shape), dtype, attrs)
        self.nodes.append(n)
        return Tensor(self, n.id)

    def tensor(self, shape, name, dtype="f32"):
        return self.add("Input", [], shape, dtype, name=name)

    def constant(self, value, dtype="f32"):
        return self.add("Constant", [], (), dtype, value=float(value))

    # ---- inspection
    def topo_order(self):
        """Node ids such that every input comes before its user (ids already are; we verify)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Graph.topo_order")

    def op_counts(self):
        raise NotImplementedError("Graph.op_counts")

    def semantics_table(self):
        """[(id, op, input ids, shape, dtype, note)] — the L1 deliverable."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Graph.semantics_table")

    def materialized_bytes(self, elem_bytes=4):
        """Bytes a naive executor would allocate: every non-view, non-input node gets a buffer."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Graph.materialized_bytes")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Tensor:
    g: Graph
    id: int

    @property
    def shape(self):
        return self.g.nodes[self.id].shape

    @property
    def dtype(self):
        return self.g.nodes[self.id].dtype

    def output(self):
        self.g.outputs.append(self.id)
        return self

    # ---- movement
    def expand(self, axis, size):
        s = list(self.shape)
        s.insert(axis, size)
        return self.g.add("Expand", [self], s, self.dtype, axis=axis, size=size)

    def expand_to(self, shape):
        """Right-aligned numpy-style broadcast by inserting leading / size-1-free axes."""
        t = self
        for axis in range(len(shape) - len(self.shape)):
            t = t.expand(axis, shape[axis])
        assert t.shape == tuple(shape), (t.shape, shape)
        return t

    def permute(self, axes):
        return self.g.add("Permute", [self], [self.shape[a] for a in axes], self.dtype, axes=tuple(axes))

    # ---- binary (shapes must already agree, like Luminal: broadcasting is explicit)
    def _bin(self, op, other, dtype=None):
        if not isinstance(other, Tensor):
            other = self.g.constant(other).expand_to(self.shape) if self.shape else self.g.constant(other)
        assert self.shape == other.shape, f"{op}: {self.shape} vs {other.shape}"
        return self.g.add(op, [self, other], self.shape, dtype or self.dtype)

    def __add__(self, o):
        return self._bin("Add", o)

    def __mul__(self, o):
        return self._bin("Mul", o)

    def lt(self, o):
        return self._bin("LessThan", o, "bool")

    def select(self, a, b):
        """self is the boolean condition."""
        assert self.shape == a.shape == b.shape
        return self.g.add("Select", [self, a, b], a.shape, a.dtype)

    def sum(self, axis):
        s = list(self.shape)
        s.pop(axis)
        return self.g.add("ReduceSum", [self], s, self.dtype, axis=axis)

    # ---- high-level ops, spelled with primitives
    def matmul(self, w):
        """[M, K] @ [K, N]: both operands become [M, N, K] views, then Mul and ReduceSum(K)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("Tensor.matmul")

    def relu(self):
        raise NotImplementedError("Tensor.relu")

    def exp(self):
        raise NotImplementedError("Tensor.exp")

    def exp2(self):
        return self.g.add("Exp2", [self], self.shape, self.dtype)


def build_mlp(M, K, N):
    """y = relu(x @ w + b). Returns (graph, x, w, b, y)."""
    raise NotImplementedError("build_mlp")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reference runtime
def run(g, feeds):
    """Execute in topological order with numpy. feeds: {input name: array}. Returns {node id: array}."""
    raise NotImplementedError("run")

验收

./check.sh l1
tests/test_l1_graph.py66 行
import numpy as np
import pytest

from drill import load

l1 = load("l1_graph")


def test_recording_does_not_compute():
    g = l1.Graph()
    x = g.tensor((2, 3), "x")
    y = x + x
    assert isinstance(y, l1.Tensor) and len(g.nodes) == 2 and g.nodes[1].op == "Add"


def test_matmul_is_broadcast_mul_reduce():
    g = l1.Graph()
    a, b = g.tensor((5, 3), "a"), g.tensor((3, 7), "b")
    c = a.matmul(b).output()
    assert c.shape == (5, 7)
    ops = [g.nodes[i].op for i in g.topo_order()]
    assert ops.count("Mul") == 1 and ops.count("ReduceSum") == 1 and ops.count("Permute") == 1
    mul = next(n for n in g.nodes if n.op == "Mul")
    assert mul.shape == (5, 7, 3)                     # the [M, N, K] product
    with pytest.raises(AssertionError):
        a.matmul(g.tensor((4, 7), "bad"))


def test_mlp_graph_shape_and_counts():
    g, x, w, b, y = l1.build_mlp(2, 3, 4)
    assert y.shape == (2, 4) and y.dtype == "f32"
    assert g.op_counts() == {"Input": 3, "Expand": 5, "Permute": 1, "Mul": 1, "ReduceSum": 1, "Add": 1,
                             "Constant": 1, "LessThan": 1, "Select": 1}
    table = g.semantics_table()
    lt = next(r for r in table if r[1] == "LessThan")
    assert lt[4] == "bool"
    assert any("view" in r[5] for r in table if r[1] == "Expand")
    assert all(set(g.nodes[r[0]].op for r in table) <= l1.PRIMITIVES for _ in [0])


def test_reference_runtime_matches_numpy():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for M, K, N in [(2, 3, 4), (1, 1, 1), (7, 13, 5)]:
        g, x, w, b, y = l1.build_mlp(M, K, N)
        X, W, B = rng.standard_normal((M, K)), rng.standard_normal((K, N)), rng.standard_normal(N)
        v = l1.run(g, {"x": X, "w": W, "b": B})
        np.testing.assert_allclose(v[y.id], np.maximum(X @ W + B, 0), rtol=1e-5, atol=1e-5)


def test_relu_is_select_and_exp_is_exp2():
    g = l1.Graph()
    x = g.tensor((3,), "x")
    r = x.relu().output()
    assert g.nodes[r.id].op == "Select"
    e = x.exp().output()
    assert g.nodes[e.id].op == "Exp2"
    v = l1.run(g, {"x": np.array([-1.0, 0.0, 2.0])})
    np.testing.assert_allclose(v[r.id], [0, 0, 2])
    np.testing.assert_allclose(v[e.id], np.exp([-1.0, 0.0, 2.0]), rtol=1e-6)


def test_naive_execution_materializes_the_product():
    g, *_ = l1.build_mlp(64, 256, 64)
    # Mul [64, 64, 256] alone is 4 MiB of fp32 — that's what fusion must avoid
    assert g.materialized_bytes() >= 64 * 64 * 256 * 4
    assert g.materialized_bytes() == (64 * 64 * 256 + 4 * 64 * 64) * 4

提示

L1matmul 的两个 view方向（轻度剧透）

A 是 [M, K]，在第 1 维插入 N：[M, N, K]。B 是 [K, N]，先转置成 [N, K]，再在第 0 维插入 M：[M, N, K]。两者逐元素相乘，沿最后一维求和得到 [M, N]。

L2run 里的 Expand拆解与常见坑（中度剧透）

np.broadcast_to(np.expand_dims(a, axis), node.shape)：先插一个长度 1 的轴，再广播成目标形状。broadcast_to 返回的是 view，不复制数据，和 Luminal 的语义一致。

L3materialized_bytes 的数几乎是答案（重度剧透）

build_mlp(64, 256, 64)：Mul 是 64·64·256 个元素，ReduceSum、Add、LessThan、Select 各 64·64 个，乘 4 字节，测试里写成 (64·64·256 + 4·64·64)·4。

参考解

解solutions/l1_graph.py先自己写完、跑过测试再看
solutions/l1_graph.py226 行
"""L1 · Graph semantics: y = relu(x @ w + b) as a Luminal-style primitive graph — reference solution.

Luminal records every tensor operation into a static DAG instead of running it (lazy, ahead of
time). High-level ops are *spelled* with a small primitive set (README: "Everything in Luminal
boils down to 15 primitive ops"; src/logical/ir.rs `LogicalOp` has the current full list):

  matmul(a, b)  = broadcast both to [M, N, K]  ->  Mul  ->  ReduceSum(K)     (src/frontend/matmul.rs)
  relu(x)       = maximum(x, 0) = Select(LessThan(x, 0), 0, x)                (src/frontend/unary.rs, binary.rs)
  exp(x)        = Exp2(x * log2(e))                                           (src/frontend/unary.rs)
  a + b[N]      = Add(a, Expand(b))  — broadcasting is a *view*, not a copy

This file is a ~150-line Python model of that design: a Graph of nodes with shapes and dtypes,
a GraphTensor API that records, a node-semantics table, and a reference runtime (numpy) that
executes the DAG in topological order — the analogue of Luminal's ReferenceRuntime.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

import numpy as np

PRIMITIVES = {"Input", "Constant", "Add", "Mul", "Recip", "Exp2", "Log2", "Sqrt", "Sin", "LessThan",
              "Select", "ReduceSum", "ReduceMax", "Expand", "Permute", "Cast"}
MOVEMENT = {"Expand", "Permute"}          # views: change indexing, move no data


@dataclass
class Node:
    id: int
    op: str
    inputs: list
    shape: tuple
    dtype: str = "f32"
    attrs: dict = field(default_factory=dict)


class Graph:
    def __init__(self):
        self.nodes = []
        self.outputs = []

    def add(self, op, inputs, shape, dtype="f32", **attrs):
        assert op in PRIMITIVES, op
        n = Node(len(self.nodes), op, [t.id for t in inputs], tuple(shape), dtype, attrs)
        self.nodes.append(n)
        return Tensor(self, n.id)

    def tensor(self, shape, name, dtype="f32"):
        return self.add("Input", [], shape, dtype, name=name)

    def constant(self, value, dtype="f32"):
        return self.add("Constant", [], (), dtype, value=float(value))

    # ---- inspection
    def topo_order(self):
        """Node ids such that every input comes before its user (ids already are; we verify)."""
        seen, order = set(), []

        def visit(i):
            if i in seen:
                return
            seen.add(i)
            for j in self.nodes[i].inputs:
                visit(j)
            order.append(i)
        for o in (self.outputs or [len(self.nodes) - 1]):
            visit(o)
        return order

    def op_counts(self):
        c = {}
        for i in self.topo_order():
            c[self.nodes[i].op] = c.get(self.nodes[i].op, 0) + 1
        return c

    def semantics_table(self):
        """[(id, op, input ids, shape, dtype, note)] — the L1 deliverable."""
        rows = []
        for i in self.topo_order():
            n = self.nodes[i]
            note = ""
            if n.op == "Expand":
                note = f"broadcast axis {n.attrs['axis']} to {n.attrs['size']} (view)"
            elif n.op == "Permute":
                note = f"axes {n.attrs['axes']} (view)"
            elif n.op in ("ReduceSum", "ReduceMax"):
                note = f"reduce axis {n.attrs['axis']}"
            elif n.op == "Input":
                note = n.attrs["name"]
            elif n.op == "Constant":
                note = repr(n.attrs["value"])
            rows.append((n.id, n.op, list(n.inputs), n.shape, n.dtype, note))
        return rows

    def materialized_bytes(self, elem_bytes=4):
        """Bytes a naive executor would allocate: every non-view, non-input node gets a buffer."""
        total = 0
        for i in self.topo_order():
            n = self.nodes[i]
            if n.op not in MOVEMENT and n.op not in ("Input", "Constant"):
                total += int(np.prod(n.shape)) * elem_bytes
        return total


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Tensor:
    g: Graph
    id: int

    @property
    def shape(self):
        return self.g.nodes[self.id].shape

    @property
    def dtype(self):
        return self.g.nodes[self.id].dtype

    def output(self):
        self.g.outputs.append(self.id)
        return self

    # ---- movement
    def expand(self, axis, size):
        s = list(self.shape)
        s.insert(axis, size)
        return self.g.add("Expand", [self], s, self.dtype, axis=axis, size=size)

    def expand_to(self, shape):
        """Right-aligned numpy-style broadcast by inserting leading / size-1-free axes."""
        t = self
        for axis in range(len(shape) - len(self.shape)):
            t = t.expand(axis, shape[axis])
        assert t.shape == tuple(shape), (t.shape, shape)
        return t

    def permute(self, axes):
        return self.g.add("Permute", [self], [self.shape[a] for a in axes], self.dtype, axes=tuple(axes))

    # ---- binary (shapes must already agree, like Luminal: broadcasting is explicit)
    def _bin(self, op, other, dtype=None):
        if not isinstance(other, Tensor):
            other = self.g.constant(other).expand_to(self.shape) if self.shape else self.g.constant(other)
        assert self.shape == other.shape, f"{op}: {self.shape} vs {other.shape}"
        return self.g.add(op, [self, other], self.shape, dtype or self.dtype)

    def __add__(self, o):
        return self._bin("Add", o)

    def __mul__(self, o):
        return self._bin("Mul", o)

    def lt(self, o):
        return self._bin("LessThan", o, "bool")

    def select(self, a, b):
        """self is the boolean condition."""
        assert self.shape == a.shape == b.shape
        return self.g.add("Select", [self, a, b], a.shape, a.dtype)

    def sum(self, axis):
        s = list(self.shape)
        s.pop(axis)
        return self.g.add("ReduceSum", [self], s, self.dtype, axis=axis)

    # ---- high-level ops, spelled with primitives
    def matmul(self, w):
        """[M, K] @ [K, N]: both operands become [M, N, K] views, then Mul and ReduceSum(K)."""
        (m, k), (k2, n) = self.shape, w.shape
        assert k == k2, "contract dim mismatch"
        a = self.expand(1, n)                      # [M, N, K]
        b = w.permute([1, 0]).expand(0, m)         # [K, N] -> [N, K] -> [M, N, K]
        return (a * b).sum(2)

    def relu(self):
        zero = self.g.constant(0.0).expand_to(self.shape)
        return self.lt(zero).select(zero, self)

    def exp(self):
        return (self * float(np.log2(np.e))).exp2()

    def exp2(self):
        return self.g.add("Exp2", [self], self.shape, self.dtype)


def build_mlp(M, K, N):
    """y = relu(x @ w + b). Returns (graph, x, w, b, y)."""
    g = Graph()
    x, w, b = g.tensor((M, K), "x"), g.tensor((K, N), "w"), g.tensor((N,), "b")
    y = (x.matmul(w) + b.expand_to((M, N))).relu().output()
    return g, x, w, b, y


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- reference runtime
def run(g, feeds):
    """Execute in topological order with numpy. feeds: {input name: array}. Returns {node id: array}."""
    vals = {}
    for i in g.topo_order():
        n = g.nodes[i]
        a = [vals[j] for j in n.inputs]
        if n.op == "Input":
            v = np.asarray(feeds[n.attrs["name"]], np.float32)
            assert v.shape == n.shape, (n.attrs["name"], v.shape, n.shape)
        elif n.op == "Constant":
            v = np.float32(n.attrs["value"])
        elif n.op == "Expand":
            v = np.broadcast_to(np.expand_dims(a[0], n.attrs["axis"]), n.shape)
        elif n.op == "Permute":
            v = np.transpose(a[0], n.attrs["axes"])
        elif n.op == "Add":
            v = a[0] + a[1]
        elif n.op == "Mul":
            v = a[0] * a[1]
        elif n.op == "LessThan":
            v = a[0] < a[1]
        elif n.op == "Select":
            v = np.where(a[0], a[1], a[2])
        elif n.op == "ReduceSum":
            v = a[0].sum(axis=n.attrs["axis"], dtype=np.float32)
        elif n.op == "ReduceMax":
            v = a[0].max(axis=n.attrs["axis"])
        elif n.op == "Exp2":
            v = np.exp2(a[0])
        elif n.op == "Recip":
            v = 1 / a[0]
        else:
            raise NotImplementedError(n.op)
        vals[i] = v
    return vals

在 Luminal 里：看真正的图

use luminal::prelude::*;
let mut cx = Graph::new();
let x = cx.tensor(vec![2, 3], DType::F32);
let w = cx.tensor(vec![3, 4], DType::F32);
let b = cx.tensor(vec![4], DType::F32);
let y = (x.matmul(w) + b.expand_rhs(vec![2, 4])).relu().output();
// 编译前后都可以导出可视化：src/visualization.rs；编译时 EGGLOG_DEBUG=1 会把 egglog 程序也打印出来

对照你的语义表：真实图里每个操作数的 broadcast 是一个 view map 节点（matmul.rs 调用 luminalrecord_view_map，注释说“Record ONE view apply ... no intermediate movement scaffolding”），而不是一串 Expand / Permute；relu 会多出 NaN 覆盖那一个 Select。这些差异正好说明：primitive 集合的设计（README 的 15 个 vs LogicalOp 的完整列表）本身也在演进，读代码时以 pin 住的 commit 为准。

自测：为什么 broadcast 不复制数据也能被“搜索”？

broadcast 只是索引映射：b_expanded[m, n] = b[n]。它在图里是一个节点，所以 e-graph 可以对它做改写（比如把 Add(ReduceSum(Mul(...)), Expand(b)) 识别成“带 bias 的 GEMM”，匹配到 cuBLASLt 的 EPILOGUE_BIAS）；而它在执行时可以不分配任何内存。把“改变索引”和“搬数据”分开，是 Luminal 能自动发现 fusion 的前提。

双主线 · Track L · Luminal（graph compiler） · 第 11 题

L2E-graph 从零写：union-find、rebuild、rewrite、saturation、extraction

Luminal 的搜索空间是一个 e-graph：所有等价程序同时存在，改写只增不删。这道题从零写一个 e-graph（hashcons、union-find、congruence rebuild、e-matching、equality saturation、带预算的停止、基于代价的 extraction），用 (a+b)+c、a+(b+c)、b+a 手算验证，再看 Luminal 为什么只对整数开放交换律。

⏱ 75 min 难度 难 你写 mine/l2_egraph.py验收 ./check.sh l2参考解 solutions/l2_egraph.py 📖 单独讲解页
  1. 背景
  2. 源码与资料
  3. 要用到的知识
  4. 浮点下哪些 rewrite 不安全
  5. 题目要求
  6. 题干
  7. 验收
  8. 提示
  9. 参考解
  10. egglog 小实验（roadmap 的交付物）

背景

roadmap 的 L2：“用 (a+b)+c、a+(b+c)、a+b、b+a 画 e-class；讨论浮点下哪些 rewrite 不一定安全。交付：e-graph 草图 + 一个 egglog 小实验。”

Luminal 的规则写在 egglog 里：核心规则在 luminalegglog_preamble.egg，每个 primitive 自己的规则在 src/logical/op/<op>/*.egg。例如 luminaladd/rewrite.egg 开头就是一段值得背下来的注释：

; Encoding bits are observable. Float operand permutation can change a NaN
; payload, so unqualified floating commutativity is not a bit-exact equality.
; Retain the law for exact integer encodings only (fail closed on unknown dtype).
(rule ((= ?out (LogicalAdd ?lhs ?rhs)) (= (dtype-of ?out) (Int)))
 ((union ?out (LogicalAdd ?rhs ?lhs)))
 :ruleset main_ruleset :name "add/rewrite: commutativity, Int")

连 a + b → b + a 都只对整数开放——更不用说结合律。

源码与资料

egg 论文：Fast and Extensible Equality Saturation · egglog 论文 · egglogegglog README · egglogpyegglog-python README · luminalpreamble 里的整数恒等式规则 · luminalextraction.rs：Genome（L3）

要用到的知识

部件 做什么
hashcons 规范化后的 e-node → e-class；同一个 ("+", c1, c2) 只存一份
union-find find(c) 给出代表元；union 合并两个类
rebuild 合并之后，子节点的代表元变了，原本不同的 e-node 可能变得相同（f(a) 和 f(b) 在 a = b 之后），要把它们所在的类也合并（congruence）
e-matching 在某个类里找所有能匹配模式 ("+", ("+", "?x", "?y"), "?z") 的替换
saturation 每轮：先收集所有规则的所有匹配，再统一 instantiate + union，然后 rebuild；一轮下来类数和节点数都没变 → 饱和
budget iter_limit、node_limit：没饱和也要停（"iter_limit" / "node_limit"）
extraction 自底向上求每个类的最小代价（要迭代到不动点，因为类可以有环：x·1 = x）
类比 · 族谱和同名的人

e-class 像“同一个人的所有称呼”：张三、老张、张经理都指向同一个人（union-find 记住“谁是谁”）。hashcons 保证“张经理的儿子”这个称呼只登记一次。rebuild 处理连锁反应：一旦发现“老张”和“张经理”是同一个人，那“老张的儿子”和“张经理的儿子”也一定是同一个人。extraction 就是在所有称呼里挑最短的那个写进正式文件。

浮点下哪些 rewrite 不安全

规则 实数上 浮点上
a + b → b + a 成立 数值上成立，但 NaN 的 payload 可能不同 → Luminal 只对整数开放
(a + b) + c → a + (b + c) 成立 不成立：(1e8 + −1e8) + 1 = 1，1e8 + (−1e8 + 1) = 0（测试 test_reassociation_is_not_bit_exact）
x · 1 → x、x + 0 → x 成立 −0.0 + 0.0 = +0.0，所以 x + 0 → x 对 x = −0.0 改变了符号位
exp2(log2(x)) → x x > 0 时成立 x ≤ 0 时左边是 NaN，右边不是
a·b + a·c → a·(b + c) 成立 舍入次数不同；也可能溢出 / 不溢出

题目要求

在 mine/l2_egraph.py 里实现（find、canon、add_enode、add、num_classes、num_nodes、规则集、两个代价函数和 evaluate 已给出）：

方法 要点
union(a, b) 小类挂到大类下面，合并 e-node 集合；返回是否真的合并了
rebuild() 重新规范化所有 e-node；两个类里出现了同一个 e-node 就合并；直到不再变化；重建 hashcons
equiv(t1, t2) 两个项加进来，比较代表元
ematch(pattern, cid, subst) 变量（?x）绑定或检查一致；叶子比对 (leaf,)；复合模式对类里每个同名同元数的 e-node 递归
instantiate(pattern, subst) 按替换把模式加进图里
run(rules, iter_limit, node_limit) 返回 {"stop": "saturated" / "iter_limit" / "node_limit", "history": [...]}
extract(cid, cost) (最小代价, 项)；cost(op, child_costs)

题干

mine/l2_egraph.py120 行
"""L2 · E-graphs and equality saturation from scratch — your implementation (run ./check.sh l2).

Luminal's search space is an e-graph built by egglog (src/egglog_core/egglog_preamble.egg holds
the rules). An e-graph stores *many equivalent programs at once*:

  e-node   an operator applied to e-classes      ("+", c1, c2)
  e-class  a set of e-nodes known to be equal    {a+b, b+a}
  rewrite  lhs -> rhs: when lhs matches in class C, add rhs and union it into C (nothing is deleted)
  rebuild  restore congruence: if a ≡ b then f(a) ≡ f(b)
  saturate apply all rules until nothing new appears (or a node / iteration budget runs out)
  extract  pick one e-node per class to minimize a cost -> one concrete program

Terms are tuples: ("+", x, y), leaves are strings or numbers. Pattern variables start with "?".
"""
from itertools import count


class EGraph:
    def __init__(self):
        self.parent = {}            # union-find over class ids
        self.classes = {}           # canonical id -> set of canonical e-nodes
        self.hashcons = {}          # canonical e-node -> class id
        self._ids = count()
        self.pending = []

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- union-find
    def find(self, a):
        while self.parent[a] != a:
            self.parent[a] = self.parent[self.parent[a]]
            a = self.parent[a]
        return a

    def canon(self, enode):
        op, *kids = enode
        return (op, *(self.find(k) for k in kids))

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- building
    def add_enode(self, enode):
        enode = self.canon(enode)
        if enode in self.hashcons:
            return self.find(self.hashcons[enode])
        cid = next(self._ids)
        self.parent[cid] = cid
        self.classes[cid] = {enode}
        self.hashcons[enode] = cid
        return cid

    def add(self, term):
        if isinstance(term, tuple):
            return self.add_enode((term[0], *(self.add(t) for t in term[1:])))
        return self.add_enode((term,))

    def union(self, a, b):
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.union")

    def rebuild(self):
        """Re-canonicalize every e-node; merge classes whose e-nodes became identical (congruence)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.rebuild")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- queries
    def equiv(self, t1, t2):
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.equiv")

    def num_classes(self):
        return len(self.classes)

    def num_nodes(self):
        return sum(len(v) for v in self.classes.values())

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- e-matching
    def ematch(self, pattern, cid, subst=None):
        """All substitutions {?var: class id} under which `pattern` matches something in class cid."""
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.ematch")

    def instantiate(self, pattern, subst):
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.instantiate")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- saturation
    def run(self, rules, iter_limit=20, node_limit=10_000):
        """Equality saturation. rules: [(name, lhs, rhs)]. Returns a report dict."""
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.run")

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- extraction
    def extract(self, cid, cost):
        """Cheapest term in class cid. cost(op, child_costs) -> number. Bottom-up fixpoint
        (classes may be cyclic, e.g. x*1 = x)."""
        raise NotImplementedError("EGraph.extract")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- rule sets
COMM_ASSOC = [
    ("comm-add", ("+", "?a", "?b"), ("+", "?b", "?a")),
    ("assoc-add", ("+", ("+", "?a", "?b"), "?c"), ("+", "?a", ("+", "?b", "?c"))),
]
ALGEBRA = COMM_ASSOC + [
    ("mul-one", ("*", "?a", 1), "?a"),
    ("add-zero", ("+", "?a", 0), "?a"),
    ("factor", ("+", ("*", "?a", "?b"), ("*", "?a", "?c")), ("*", "?a", ("+", "?b", "?c"))),
    ("exp2-log2", ("exp2", ("log2", "?a")), "?a"),
]


def ast_size(op, kids):
    return 1 + sum(kids)


def op_cost(op, kids):
    """Rough kernel cost: multiplies cost more than adds; leaves are free."""
    return {"*": 4, "+": 1, "exp2": 8, "log2": 8}.get(op, 0) + sum(kids)


def evaluate(term, env, dtype=float):
    """Evaluate a term with Python floats or numpy scalars — to show which rewrites change bits."""
    import math
    if isinstance(term, tuple):
        op, *k = term
        v = [evaluate(t, env, dtype) for t in k]
        return {"+": lambda a, b: dtype(a + b), "*": lambda a, b: dtype(a * b),
                "exp2": lambda a: dtype(2.0 ** a), "log2": lambda a: dtype(math.log2(a))}[op](*v)
    return dtype(env[term]) if isinstance(term, str) else dtype(term)

验收

./check.sh l2
tests/test_l2_egraph.py90 行
import numpy as np

from drill import load

l2 = load("l2_egraph")
E = l2.EGraph


def test_hashcons_shares_subterms():
    g = E()
    a = g.add(("+", "a", "b"))
    b = g.add(("+", "a", "b"))
    assert a == b and g.num_classes() == 3 and g.num_nodes() == 3


def test_union_and_congruence():
    g = E()
    fa, fb = g.add(("f", "a")), g.add(("f", "b"))
    assert g.find(fa) != g.find(fb)
    g.union(g.add("a"), g.add("b"))
    g.rebuild()
    assert g.find(fa) == g.find(fb)          # a = b  =>  f(a) = f(b)


def test_ematch():
    g = E()
    root = g.add(("+", ("+", "a", "b"), "c"))
    subs = g.ematch(("+", ("+", "?x", "?y"), "?z"), root)
    assert len(subs) == 1 and g.find(subs[0]["?x"]) == g.find(g.add("a"))
    assert g.ematch(("+", "?x", "?x"), root) == []
    assert len(g.ematch(("+", "?x", "?x"), g.add(("+", "a", "a")))) == 1


def test_three_term_saturation_counts():
    g = E()
    g.add(("+", ("+", "a", "b"), "c"))
    rep = g.run(l2.COMM_ASSOC)
    assert rep["stop"] == "saturated"
    # classes: a, b, c, a+b, a+c, b+c, a+b+c ; e-nodes: 3 leaves + 2 per pair + 6 at the top
    assert g.num_classes() == 7 and g.num_nodes() == 15
    assert g.equiv(("+", "a", ("+", "b", "c")), ("+", ("+", "a", "b"), "c"))
    assert g.equiv(("+", ("+", "c", "a"), "b"), ("+", "b", ("+", "c", "a")))
    assert not g.equiv(("+", "a", "b"), ("+", "a", "c"))


def test_blowup_is_exponential():
    sizes = []
    for n in (3, 4, 5, 6):
        g = E()
        t = "x0"
        for i in range(1, n):
            t = ("+", t, f"x{i}")
        g.add(t)
        assert g.run(l2.COMM_ASSOC, iter_limit=30, node_limit=10 ** 6)["stop"] == "saturated"
        assert g.num_classes() == 2 ** n - 1
        assert g.num_nodes() == 3 ** n - 2 ** (n + 1) + 1 + n
        sizes.append(g.num_nodes())
    assert sizes == [15, 54, 185, 608]


def test_budgets_stop_the_search():
    g = E()
    t = "x0"
    for i in range(1, 8):
        t = ("+", t, f"x{i}")
    g.add(t)
    assert g.run(l2.COMM_ASSOC, iter_limit=2)["stop"] == "iter_limit"
    g2 = E()
    g2.add(t)
    assert g2.run(l2.COMM_ASSOC, iter_limit=50, node_limit=500)["stop"] == "node_limit"


def test_extraction_picks_the_cheaper_program():
    g = E()
    root = g.add(("+", ("*", "x", "y"), ("*", "x", "z")))
    g.run(l2.ALGEBRA)
    cost, term = g.extract(root, l2.op_cost)
    assert cost == 5 and term[0] == "*" and g.equiv(term, ("*", "x", ("+", "y", "z")))
    g = E()
    root = g.add(("*", ("exp2", ("log2", "x")), 1))
    g.run(l2.ALGEBRA)
    assert g.extract(root, l2.op_cost) == (0, "x")
    assert g.extract(root, l2.ast_size) == (1, "x")


def test_reassociation_is_not_bit_exact():
    env = {"a": 1e8, "b": -1e8, "c": 1.0}
    left = l2.evaluate(("+", ("+", "a", "b"), "c"), env, np.float32)
    right = l2.evaluate(("+", "a", ("+", "b", "c")), env, np.float32)
    assert left == 1.0 and right == 0.0      # equal in the e-graph, different in fp32

提示

L1先过 congruence 测试方向（轻度剧透）

union 之后不要立刻修 hashcons；全部 union 完再调一次 rebuild()。rebuild 的循环：把每个类的 e-node 集合重新 canon；用一个新字典 enode → 类，遇到已经属于另一个类的同一个 e-node 就 union，并记下“这一轮有变化”。

L2一轮饱和的顺序拆解与常见坑（中度剧透）

先对所有规则、所有类收集匹配（matches），再统一 instantiate + union，最后 rebuild。边匹配边修改会让同一轮的结果依赖遍历顺序。停止条件：这一轮 unions == 0 且类数、节点数都和这一轮开始时一样。

L3extraction 的不动点几乎是答案（重度剧透）

best = {}；反复扫描所有类的所有 e-node：子类都已有代价时，算出这个 e-node 的代价，比当前更小就更新、并标记 changed。没有变化时停止。最后从根类开始，按 best 里记下的 e-node 递归构造项。

参考解

解solutions/l2_egraph.py先自己写完、跑过测试再看
solutions/l2_egraph.py201 行
"""L2 · E-graphs and equality saturation from scratch — reference solution.

Luminal's search space is an e-graph built by egglog (src/egglog_core/egglog_preamble.egg holds
the rules). An e-graph stores *many equivalent programs at once*:

  e-node   an operator applied to e-classes      ("+", c1, c2)
  e-class  a set of e-nodes known to be equal    {a+b, b+a}
  rewrite  lhs -> rhs: when lhs matches in class C, add rhs and union it into C (nothing is deleted)
  rebuild  restore congruence: if a ≡ b then f(a) ≡ f(b)
  saturate apply all rules until nothing new appears (or a node / iteration budget runs out)
  extract  pick one e-node per class to minimize a cost -> one concrete program

Terms are tuples: ("+", x, y), leaves are strings or numbers. Pattern variables start with "?".
"""
from itertools import count


class EGraph:
    def __init__(self):
        self.parent = {}            # union-find over class ids
        self.classes = {}           # canonical id -> set of canonical e-nodes
        self.hashcons = {}          # canonical e-node -> class id
        self._ids = count()
        self.pending = []

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- union-find
    def find(self, a):
        while self.parent[a] != a:
            self.parent[a] = self.parent[self.parent[a]]
            a = self.parent[a]
        return a

    def canon(self, enode):
        op, *kids = enode
        return (op, *(self.find(k) for k in kids))

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- building
    def add_enode(self, enode):
        enode = self.canon(enode)
        if enode in self.hashcons:
            return self.find(self.hashcons[enode])
        cid = next(self._ids)
        self.parent[cid] = cid
        self.classes[cid] = {enode}
        self.hashcons[enode] = cid
        return cid

    def add(self, term):
        if isinstance(term, tuple):
            return self.add_enode((term[0], *(self.add(t) for t in term[1:])))
        return self.add_enode((term,))

    def union(self, a, b):
        a, b = self.find(a), self.find(b)
        if a == b:
            return False
        if len(self.classes[a]) < len(self.classes[b]):
            a, b = b, a
        self.parent[b] = a
        self.classes[a] |= self.classes.pop(b)
        self.pending.append(a)
        return True

    def rebuild(self):
        """Re-canonicalize every e-node; merge classes whose e-nodes became identical (congruence)."""
        changed = True
        while changed:
            changed = False
            new_hash = {}
            for cid in list(self.classes):
                cid = self.find(cid)
                if cid not in self.classes:
                    continue
                self.classes[cid] = {self.canon(n) for n in self.classes[cid]}
            for cid, nodes in list(self.classes.items()):
                for n in nodes:
                    other = new_hash.get(n)
                    if other is not None and self.find(other) != self.find(cid):
                        self.union(other, cid)
                        changed = True
                    new_hash[n] = self.find(cid)
            self.hashcons = {self.canon(n): self.find(c) for n, c in new_hash.items()}
        self.pending.clear()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- queries
    def equiv(self, t1, t2):
        return self.find(self.add(t1)) == self.find(self.add(t2))

    def num_classes(self):
        return len(self.classes)

    def num_nodes(self):
        return sum(len(v) for v in self.classes.values())

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- e-matching
    def ematch(self, pattern, cid, subst=None):
        """All substitutions {?var: class id} under which `pattern` matches something in class cid."""
        subst = dict(subst or {})
        cid = self.find(cid)
        if isinstance(pattern, str) and pattern.startswith("?"):
            if pattern in subst:
                return [subst] if self.find(subst[pattern]) == cid else []
            subst[pattern] = cid
            return [subst]
        if not isinstance(pattern, tuple):
            return [subst] if (pattern,) in self.classes[cid] else []
        out = []
        op, *pkids = pattern
        for n in self.classes[cid]:
            if n[0] != op or len(n) - 1 != len(pkids):
                continue
            partial = [subst]
            for p, k in zip(pkids, n[1:]):
                partial = [s2 for s in partial for s2 in self.ematch(p, k, s)]
            out.extend(partial)
        return out

    def instantiate(self, pattern, subst):
        if isinstance(pattern, str) and pattern.startswith("?"):
            return subst[pattern]
        if not isinstance(pattern, tuple):
            return self.add_enode((pattern,))
        return self.add_enode((pattern[0], *(self.instantiate(p, subst) for p in pattern[1:])))

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- saturation
    def run(self, rules, iter_limit=20, node_limit=10_000):
        """Equality saturation. rules: [(name, lhs, rhs)]. Returns a report dict."""
        history = []
        for it in range(iter_limit):
            before = (self.num_classes(), self.num_nodes())
            matches = []
            for name, lhs, rhs in rules:
                for cid in list(self.classes):
                    for s in self.ematch(lhs, cid):
                        matches.append((name, cid, rhs, s))
            unions = 0
            for name, cid, rhs, s in matches:
                unions += self.union(cid, self.instantiate(rhs, s))
            self.rebuild()
            history.append({"iter": it, "matches": len(matches), "unions": unions,
                            "classes": self.num_classes(), "nodes": self.num_nodes()})
            if unions == 0 and (self.num_classes(), self.num_nodes()) == before:
                return {"stop": "saturated", "history": history}
            if self.num_nodes() > node_limit:
                return {"stop": "node_limit", "history": history}
        return {"stop": "iter_limit", "history": history}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------- extraction
    def extract(self, cid, cost):
        """Cheapest term in class cid. cost(op, child_costs) -> number. Bottom-up fixpoint
        (classes may be cyclic, e.g. x*1 = x)."""
        best = {}
        changed = True
        while changed:
            changed = False
            for c, nodes in self.classes.items():
                for n in nodes:
                    if all(self.find(k) in best for k in n[1:]):
                        cc = cost(n[0], [best[self.find(k)][0] for k in n[1:]])
                        if c not in best or cc < best[c][0]:
                            best[c] = (cc, n)
                            changed = True

        def build(c):
            _, n = best[self.find(c)]
            return n[0] if len(n) == 1 else (n[0], *(build(k) for k in n[1:]))
        c = self.find(cid)
        return best[c][0], build(c)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- rule sets
COMM_ASSOC = [
    ("comm-add", ("+", "?a", "?b"), ("+", "?b", "?a")),
    ("assoc-add", ("+", ("+", "?a", "?b"), "?c"), ("+", "?a", ("+", "?b", "?c"))),
]
ALGEBRA = COMM_ASSOC + [
    ("mul-one", ("*", "?a", 1), "?a"),
    ("add-zero", ("+", "?a", 0), "?a"),
    ("factor", ("+", ("*", "?a", "?b"), ("*", "?a", "?c")), ("*", "?a", ("+", "?b", "?c"))),
    ("exp2-log2", ("exp2", ("log2", "?a")), "?a"),
]


def ast_size(op, kids):
    return 1 + sum(kids)


def op_cost(op, kids):
    """Rough kernel cost: multiplies cost more than adds; leaves are free."""
    return {"*": 4, "+": 1, "exp2": 8, "log2": 8}.get(op, 0) + sum(kids)


def evaluate(term, env, dtype=float):
    """Evaluate a term with Python floats or numpy scalars — to show which rewrites change bits."""
    import math
    if isinstance(term, tuple):
        op, *k = term
        v = [evaluate(t, env, dtype) for t in k]
        return {"+": lambda a, b: dtype(a + b), "*": lambda a, b: dtype(a * b),
                "exp2": lambda a: dtype(2.0 ** a), "log2": lambda a: dtype(math.log2(a))}[op](*v)
    return dtype(env[term]) if isinstance(term, str) else dtype(term)

egglog 小实验（roadmap 的交付物）

仓库里的 examples/l2_egglog_experiment.py 用 egglog 的 Python 绑定做同一件事：声明 Num 类型和 +，注册交换律、结合律，run(10)，再 check(eq(left).to(right))。

.venv/bin/pip install egglog && .venv/bin/python examples/l2_egglog_experiment.py
# equal after saturation
# Num.var("a") + Num.var("b") + Num.var("c")
# 4 iterations

4 轮之后饱和，和你自己的 e-graph 的 history 长度一样。接着试两件事：(1) 把项数加到 6，观察 egglog 的运行时间和你的 num_nodes() 是否同样按 3ⁿ 增长；(2) 仿照 Luminal 的 add/rewrite.egg，给规则加一个条件（只在 dtype 是整数时触发），体会“fail closed”。

自测：既然浮点结合律不成立，Luminal 怎么发现 split-K 之类需要重排归约的优化？

不靠一条通用的“浮点结合律”规则，而是：(1) 把归约本身做成带语义的节点（ReduceSum、UnspecifiedOrderScanSum——名字里就写着“求值顺序未指定”），让“顺序可以变”成为这个 op 的契约，而不是任意 Add 链的性质；(2) 由用户在图里显式写出精度（matmul 不自动 cast）；(3) 用 L5 那样的 Reference vs device 对拍，在容差内验证结果。规则的合法性在图的语义层面保证，而不是对所有浮点表达式开放。

双主线 · Track L · Luminal（graph compiler） · 第 12 题

L3Search：genome、GA、实测排名、finalists 与代理成本

饱和之后 e-graph 里有成千上万个等价程序，选哪一个？Luminal 的 CUDA backend 不信成本公式，而是编译、运行、计时。这道题实现它的搜索骨架：genome（每个 e-class 选一个 e-node）、遗传算法、按实测纳秒的 rank_insert、带验证的 finalists 遍历；再把“代理成本”和“实测”放在一起，量出代理的相关性和它会犯的错。

⏱ 50 min 难度 中 你写 mine/l3_search.py验收 ./check.sh l3参考解 solutions/l3_search.py 📖 单独讲解页
  1. 背景
  2. 源码与资料
  3. 要用到的知识
  4. 题目要求
  5. 题干
  6. 验收
  7. 提示
  8. 参考解
  9. 在 Luminal 里：search trade-off 报告

背景

roadmap 的 L3：“对一个小 graph 改变可用 rewrite / 搜索预算 / timeout，记录候选数、搜索时间、最终 latency。交付：search trade-off 报告。”读源码要区分三样东西：cost proxy、compile / search time、steady-state runtime。

真实代码里的对应物：

  • luminalGenome：“per-LayoutTensor-class producer selection”，一个 ClassId → ProducerChoice 的映射。
  • luminalrank_insert：只保留 keep 个最快的 (nanos, genome)，按纳秒升序。
  • luminalPendingFinalist：“1-BASED rank in the GA's ordering”，metric 是“measured nanoseconds”；一个 finalist 被拒绝（例如 arena 超过设备内存预算），就走到下一名。
  • luminalsearch_implementations：整个流程，开头就检查“candidate search requires the device feature and a CUDA GPU”。

源码与资料

luminalCompileOptions · README：SEARCH_LOG、LUMINAL_SEARCH_SEED、SEARCH_MEMORY_MIB、LUMINAL_SEARCH_OP_NAMES · luminalcuBLASLt 进入搜索空间的方式

要用到的知识

概念 本题 要点
预算 budget：最多测几个不同的 genome 重复的 genome 不再测（fingerprint 去重）
best-so-far 曲线 curve 单调不增；它的形状告诉你“再多搜一会儿值不值”
代理成本 mlp_proxy FLOP / 字节模型，分不清手写 tile 和 cuBLASLt，看不见 layout
相关性 spearman(proxy, measured) 本例约 0.82：大方向对，细节错
finalists select_finalist fp16 累加在 K=256 时数值不合格，primitive_loop 的 arena 超预算
break-even break_even_runs 多花的搜索时间要执行多少次才能赚回来
类比 · 选餐厅

代理成本像看点评网站的评分：大致靠谱（米其林不会比路边摊差），但分不清两家都是 4.5 分的店哪家今天更好吃。实测搜索像真的去吃：准，但每家都要花一顿饭的时间（预算）。finalists 像订位：排名第一的那家今天满座（验证失败，内存超预算），那就去第二名。break-even 是在问：为了每顿饭好吃一点点，花一个月去试遍全城的餐厅，你要在这个城市住多久才划算？

题目要求

在 mine/l3_search.py 里实现（空间工具 space_size / all_genomes / fingerprint / random_genome / mutate / crossover，以及 mlp_* 工作负载已给出）：

函数 要点
rank_insert(ranked, nanos, genome, keep) 移植 search.rs：满了且不比最差的快就不插；插入位置是第一个比它慢的位置；截断到 keep
genetic_search(space, measure, budget, population, keep, seed, mutation) 先测 population 个随机 genome，然后循环：从 ranked 里选两个父代 → crossover → mutate → 测（已测过的跳过）；连续 50 次没有新 genome 就停
proxy_search(space, proxy, measure, k) 按代理排序全部 genome，只测前 k 个
spearman(xs, ys) 秩相关，并列取平均秩
select_finalist(ranked, validate) (rank, nanos, genome, rejected)；全部被拒抛 RuntimeError("no finalist ...")
break_even_runs(search_a, lat_a, search_b, lat_b) (search_a − search_b) / (lat_b − lat_a)；a 不更快时返回 inf

题干

mine/l3_search.py127 行
"""L3 · Search cost vs measured latency: a Luminal-style genome search — your implementation (run ./check.sh l3).

After saturation the e-graph holds many programs. Luminal's CUDA backend does not trust a cost
formula to choose between them; it *measures*. The pieces (crates/luminal_cuda_lite/src/search.rs,
finalists.rs, src/extraction.rs):

  Genome      one producer choice per e-class (which e-node implements this class)
  GA          a genetic search over genomes; each candidate is compiled + run, metric = measured ns
  rank_insert keep only the best `keep` genomes, sorted by measured ns
  finalists   walk the ranking; a candidate that fails validation (e.g. its arena exceeds the
              device memory budget) is rejected and the next rank is tried

Here the "e-graph" is a dict {class: [option, ...]} and `measure(genome)` is injectable.
`mlp_space()` is a small but honest model of y = relu(x @ w + b) on a GPU.
"""
import random
from itertools import product
from math import prod


def space_size(space):
    return prod(len(v) for v in space.values())


def all_genomes(space):
    keys = sorted(space)
    return [dict(zip(keys, combo)) for combo in product(*(space[k] for k in keys))]


def fingerprint(genome):
    return tuple(sorted(genome.items()))


def random_genome(space, rng):
    return {k: rng.choice(v) for k, v in sorted(space.items())}


def mutate(genome, space, rng, p=0.3):
    g = dict(genome)
    for k in sorted(space):
        if rng.random() < p:
            g[k] = rng.choice(space[k])
    return g


def crossover(a, b, rng):
    return {k: (a if rng.random() < 0.5 else b)[k] for k in sorted(a)}


def rank_insert(ranked, nanos, genome, keep):
    """Port of search.rs rank_insert: ranked is [(nanos, genome)] ascending, at most `keep` long."""
    raise NotImplementedError("rank_insert")


def genetic_search(space, measure, budget, population=6, keep=4, seed=0, mutation=0.3):
    """Measure at most `budget` distinct genomes. Returns ranked finalists, the number of distinct
    measurements, and the best-so-far curve (one entry per measurement)."""
    raise NotImplementedError("genetic_search")


def proxy_search(space, proxy, measure, k):
    """The cheap alternative: rank *every* genome by a cost proxy, measure only the top k."""
    raise NotImplementedError("proxy_search")


def spearman(xs, ys):
    """Rank correlation (no tie correction needed for the tests; ties get average rank)."""
    raise NotImplementedError("spearman")


def select_finalist(ranked, validate):
    """finalists.rs: walk ranks 1, 2, ...; return (rank, nanos, genome, rejected[]) of the first
    candidate that validates. validate(genome) -> None if OK, else a reason string."""
    raise NotImplementedError("select_finalist")


def break_even_runs(search_seconds_a, latency_a, search_seconds_b, latency_b):
    """How many executions until spending more search time (a) pays off over b. inf if never."""
    raise NotImplementedError("break_even_runs")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the workload
def mlp_space():
    """Decision points for y = relu(x @ w + b) after saturation (simplified)."""
    return {
        "matmul": ["primitive_loop", "tiled_kernel", "cublaslt"],
        "epilogue": ["separate", "fused"],
        "w_layout": ["row_major", "col_major"],
        "accumulate": ["fp32", "fp16"],
    }


MATMUL_NS = {"primitive_loop": 2400, "tiled_kernel": 130, "cublaslt": 95}
FUSE_SAVING = {"primitive_loop": 0, "tiled_kernel": 25, "cublaslt": 22}     # cuBLASLt: EPILOGUE_RELU_BIAS


def mlp_measure(noise=0.0, seed=0):
    """'Measured' ns: what a GPU would show, including effects a FLOP proxy cannot see."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)

    def m(g):
        ns = MATMUL_NS[g["matmul"]]
        ns += 30 if g["epilogue"] == "separate" else 30 - FUSE_SAVING[g["matmul"]]
        if g["matmul"] == "tiled_kernel" and g["w_layout"] == "row_major":
            ns += 20                      # B tile loads along K are strided for this kernel
        if g["accumulate"] == "fp16":
            ns -= 8
        return ns * (1 + rng.gauss(0, noise)) if noise else ns
    return m


def mlp_proxy(g):
    """A FLOP/byte proxy: knows the primitive loop is bad and fusion saves a pass, but cannot
    tell a hand-written tile from cuBLASLt, nor see layout effects."""
    base = {"primitive_loop": 2000, "tiled_kernel": 100, "cublaslt": 100}[g["matmul"]]
    return base + (30 if g["epilogue"] == "separate" else 10) + (-5 if g["accumulate"] == "fp16" else 0)


def mlp_validate(budget_bytes=8 << 20, M=64, N=64, K=256, max_err_ulps=None):
    """Reject plans whose intermediates exceed the arena budget, and fp16 accumulation (precision)."""
    def v(g):
        if g["matmul"] == "primitive_loop" and M * N * K * 4 > budget_bytes:
            return f"arena {M * N * K * 4} B > budget {budget_bytes} B"
        if g["accumulate"] == "fp16":
            return "fp16 accumulation fails the K=%d tolerance" % K
        return None
    return v

验收

./check.sh l3
tests/test_l3_search.py72 行
import pytest

from drill import load

l3 = load("l3_search")
S = l3.mlp_space()


def test_space():
    assert l3.space_size(S) == 3 * 2 * 2 * 2
    gs = l3.all_genomes(S)
    assert len(gs) == 24 and len({l3.fingerprint(g) for g in gs}) == 24


def test_rank_insert_keeps_best_k_sorted():
    r = []
    for ns, name in [(50, "a"), (30, "b"), (70, "c"), (10, "d"), (60, "e")]:
        l3.rank_insert(r, ns, name, keep=3)
    assert r == [(10, "d"), (30, "b"), (50, "a")]
    l3.rank_insert(r, 50, "tie", keep=3)        # cannot displace an equal worst
    assert r[-1] == (50, "a")


def test_true_optimum():
    m = l3.mlp_measure()
    best = min(l3.all_genomes(S), key=m)
    assert m(best) == 95 and best["matmul"] == "cublaslt" and best["epilogue"] == "fused"


def test_genetic_search_budget_and_curve():
    m = l3.mlp_measure()
    r = l3.genetic_search(S, m, budget=10, seed=0)
    assert r["measured"] == 10 and len(r["curve"]) == 10
    assert all(a >= b for a, b in zip(r["curve"], r["curve"][1:]))     # best-so-far never gets worse
    assert r["ranked"][0][0] == 95 and len(r["ranked"]) == 4
    small = l3.genetic_search(S, m, budget=3, seed=1)
    assert small["measured"] == 3


def test_proxy_is_correlated_but_not_exact():
    m = l3.mlp_measure()
    gs = l3.all_genomes(S)
    rho = l3.spearman([l3.mlp_proxy(g) for g in gs], [m(g) for g in gs])
    assert 0.7 < rho < 0.95
    assert l3.spearman([1, 2, 3], [10, 20, 30]) == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert l3.spearman([1, 2, 3], [3, 2, 1]) == pytest.approx(-1.0)


def test_finalists_walk_past_invalid_candidates():
    m = l3.mlp_measure()
    r = l3.genetic_search(S, m, budget=24, seed=0)
    rank, ns, g, rejected = l3.select_finalist(r["ranked"], l3.mlp_validate())
    assert rank == 3 and ns == 103 and g["accumulate"] == "fp32" and len(rejected) == 2
    assert all("fp16" in why for _, why in rejected)


def test_proxy_top_k_can_all_be_invalid():
    ranked = l3.proxy_search(S, l3.mlp_proxy, l3.mlp_measure(), k=3)
    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="no finalist"):
        l3.select_finalist(ranked, l3.mlp_validate())


def test_primitive_loop_breaks_the_arena_budget():
    v = l3.mlp_validate(budget_bytes=1 << 20)
    g = {"matmul": "primitive_loop", "epilogue": "separate", "w_layout": "row_major", "accumulate": "fp32"}
    assert "arena" in v(g)


def test_break_even():
    # 2 s of search to save 30 ns per call vs 0.1 s of search
    assert l3.break_even_runs(2.0, 87e-9, 0.1, 117e-9) == pytest.approx(1.9 / 30e-9)
    assert l3.break_even_runs(1.0, 120e-9, 0.1, 117e-9) == float("inf")

提示

L1rank_insert 的等号方向（轻度剧透）

nanos >= ranked[-1][0] 时不插（和 Rust 版一致：相等也不能挤掉别人）；插入位置用 nanos < metric 找第一个，所以相等的新来者排在已有者之后。

L2genetic_search 的计数拆解与常见坑（中度剧透）

用字典 seen[fingerprint] = ns 同时做缓存和计数；evaluate 在 len(seen) >= budget 时直接返回。每测一个新 genome 就 rank_insert 并把 ranked[0][0] 追加到 curve。测试用 seed=0, budget=10 检查你恰好测了 10 个、曲线单调、找到了 95 ns 的最优。

参考解

解solutions/l3_search.py先自己写完、跑过测试再看
solutions/l3_search.py186 行
"""L3 · Search cost vs measured latency: a Luminal-style genome search — reference solution.

After saturation the e-graph holds many programs. Luminal's CUDA backend does not trust a cost
formula to choose between them; it *measures*. The pieces (crates/luminal_cuda_lite/src/search.rs,
finalists.rs, src/extraction.rs):

  Genome      one producer choice per e-class (which e-node implements this class)
  GA          a genetic search over genomes; each candidate is compiled + run, metric = measured ns
  rank_insert keep only the best `keep` genomes, sorted by measured ns
  finalists   walk the ranking; a candidate that fails validation (e.g. its arena exceeds the
              device memory budget) is rejected and the next rank is tried

Here the "e-graph" is a dict {class: [option, ...]} and `measure(genome)` is injectable.
`mlp_space()` is a small but honest model of y = relu(x @ w + b) on a GPU.
"""
import random
from itertools import product
from math import prod


def space_size(space):
    return prod(len(v) for v in space.values())


def all_genomes(space):
    keys = sorted(space)
    return [dict(zip(keys, combo)) for combo in product(*(space[k] for k in keys))]


def fingerprint(genome):
    return tuple(sorted(genome.items()))


def random_genome(space, rng):
    return {k: rng.choice(v) for k, v in sorted(space.items())}


def mutate(genome, space, rng, p=0.3):
    g = dict(genome)
    for k in sorted(space):
        if rng.random() < p:
            g[k] = rng.choice(space[k])
    return g


def crossover(a, b, rng):
    return {k: (a if rng.random() < 0.5 else b)[k] for k in sorted(a)}


def rank_insert(ranked, nanos, genome, keep):
    """Port of search.rs rank_insert: ranked is [(nanos, genome)] ascending, at most `keep` long."""
    keep = max(keep, 1)
    if len(ranked) >= keep and nanos >= ranked[-1][0]:
        return
    pos = next((i for i, (m, _) in enumerate(ranked) if nanos < m), len(ranked))
    ranked.insert(pos, (nanos, genome))
    del ranked[keep:]


def genetic_search(space, measure, budget, population=6, keep=4, seed=0, mutation=0.3):
    """Measure at most `budget` distinct genomes. Returns ranked finalists, the number of distinct
    measurements, and the best-so-far curve (one entry per measurement)."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    seen, ranked, curve = {}, [], []

    def evaluate(g):
        fp = fingerprint(g)
        if fp in seen or len(seen) >= budget:
            return seen.get(fp)
        ns = measure(g)
        seen[fp] = ns
        rank_insert(ranked, ns, g, keep)
        curve.append(ranked[0][0])
        return ns

    pop = [random_genome(space, rng) for _ in range(population)]
    for g in pop:
        evaluate(g)
    stale = 0
    while len(seen) < min(budget, space_size(space)) and stale < 50:
        parents = [g for _, g in ranked] or pop
        a, b = rng.choice(parents), rng.choice(parents)
        child = mutate(crossover(a, b, rng), space, rng, mutation)
        before = len(seen)
        evaluate(child)
        stale = stale + 1 if len(seen) == before else 0
    return {"ranked": ranked, "measured": len(seen), "curve": curve}


def proxy_search(space, proxy, measure, k):
    """The cheap alternative: rank *every* genome by a cost proxy, measure only the top k."""
    cand = sorted(all_genomes(space), key=proxy)[:k]
    ranked = []
    for g in cand:
        rank_insert(ranked, measure(g), g, k)
    return ranked


def spearman(xs, ys):
    """Rank correlation (no tie correction needed for the tests; ties get average rank)."""
    def ranks(v):
        order = sorted(range(len(v)), key=lambda i: v[i])
        r = [0.0] * len(v)
        i = 0
        while i < len(order):
            j = i
            while j + 1 < len(order) and v[order[j + 1]] == v[order[i]]:
                j += 1
            for t in range(i, j + 1):
                r[order[t]] = (i + j) / 2
            i = j + 1
        return r
    rx, ry = ranks(xs), ranks(ys)
    n = len(xs)
    mx, my = sum(rx) / n, sum(ry) / n
    cov = sum((a - mx) * (b - my) for a, b in zip(rx, ry))
    vx = sum((a - mx) ** 2 for a in rx) ** 0.5
    vy = sum((b - my) ** 2 for b in ry) ** 0.5
    return cov / (vx * vy)


def select_finalist(ranked, validate):
    """finalists.rs: walk ranks 1, 2, ...; return (rank, nanos, genome, rejected[]) of the first
    candidate that validates. validate(genome) -> None if OK, else a reason string."""
    rejected = []
    for rank, (ns, g) in enumerate(ranked, 1):
        why = validate(g)
        if why is None:
            return rank, ns, g, rejected
        rejected.append((rank, why))
    raise RuntimeError(f"no finalist validated: {rejected}")


def break_even_runs(search_seconds_a, latency_a, search_seconds_b, latency_b):
    """How many executions until spending more search time (a) pays off over b. inf if never."""
    if latency_a >= latency_b:
        return float("inf")
    return (search_seconds_a - search_seconds_b) / (latency_b - latency_a)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the workload
def mlp_space():
    """Decision points for y = relu(x @ w + b) after saturation (simplified)."""
    return {
        "matmul": ["primitive_loop", "tiled_kernel", "cublaslt"],
        "epilogue": ["separate", "fused"],
        "w_layout": ["row_major", "col_major"],
        "accumulate": ["fp32", "fp16"],
    }


MATMUL_NS = {"primitive_loop": 2400, "tiled_kernel": 130, "cublaslt": 95}
FUSE_SAVING = {"primitive_loop": 0, "tiled_kernel": 25, "cublaslt": 22}     # cuBLASLt: EPILOGUE_RELU_BIAS


def mlp_measure(noise=0.0, seed=0):
    """'Measured' ns: what a GPU would show, including effects a FLOP proxy cannot see."""
    rng = random.Random(seed)

    def m(g):
        ns = MATMUL_NS[g["matmul"]]
        ns += 30 if g["epilogue"] == "separate" else 30 - FUSE_SAVING[g["matmul"]]
        if g["matmul"] == "tiled_kernel" and g["w_layout"] == "row_major":
            ns += 20                      # B tile loads along K are strided for this kernel
        if g["accumulate"] == "fp16":
            ns -= 8
        return ns * (1 + rng.gauss(0, noise)) if noise else ns
    return m


def mlp_proxy(g):
    """A FLOP/byte proxy: knows the primitive loop is bad and fusion saves a pass, but cannot
    tell a hand-written tile from cuBLASLt, nor see layout effects."""
    base = {"primitive_loop": 2000, "tiled_kernel": 100, "cublaslt": 100}[g["matmul"]]
    return base + (30 if g["epilogue"] == "separate" else 10) + (-5 if g["accumulate"] == "fp16" else 0)


def mlp_validate(budget_bytes=8 << 20, M=64, N=64, K=256, max_err_ulps=None):
    """Reject plans whose intermediates exceed the arena budget, and fp16 accumulation (precision)."""
    def v(g):
        if g["matmul"] == "primitive_loop" and M * N * K * 4 > budget_bytes:
            return f"arena {M * N * K * 4} B > budget {budget_bytes} B"
        if g["accumulate"] == "fp16":
            return "fp16 accumulation fails the K=%d tolerance" % K
        return None
    return v

在 Luminal 里：search trade-off 报告

# 在 CUDA 机器上，对一个小 graph（例如 crates/luminal_cuda_lite/examples 里最小的那个，或你自己的 MLP）
SEARCH_LOG=1 LUMINAL_SEARCH_OP_NAMES=1 LUMINAL_SEARCH_SEED=0 cargo run --release -p luminal_cuda_lite --example <name> --features device 2>&1 | tee search.log
SEARCH_LOG=1 LUMINAL_SEARCH_SEED=1 ...    # 换种子
SEARCH_MEMORY_MIB=256 ...                 # 收紧内存预算，看 finalist 拒绝 / fallback 行

报告里要有：每次运行的候选数、搜索时间、最终 latency、best-so-far 曲线（从 SEARCH_LOG 的 best-so-far 行整理）、被拒的 finalist 及原因，以及一个 break-even 估计。以当前 README 和 CompileOptions 实际支持的开关为准——如果某个参数（比如搜索预算）只能在代码里改，就在报告里注明改了哪一行。

自测：为什么 proxy_search 的前 3 名全军覆没，而 GA 找到了合格的第 3 名？

代理成本认为 fp16 累加更便宜（−5），所以它的前 3 名全是 fp16 累加；但 validation 认为 fp16 在 K=256 时不满足容差，全部拒绝。GA 实测了更多样的 genome，ranked 里保留了 fp32 的候选，finalist 遍历到第 3 名时找到了合格的 103 ns 方案。教训：排名和验证要分开，且排名之后要留足够多的候选——这正是 finalists.rs 存在的原因。

双主线 · Track L · Luminal（graph compiler） · 第 13 题

L4Backend trace：选中计划 → launch 列表 → arena 分配（plan_slab）

搜索选中了一个计划之后，backend 还要做两件事：决定每个 buffer 放在一块设备内存的哪个偏移上，以及按依赖顺序发出 launch。这道题移植 Luminal 的 plan_slab（DAG 可达性 + first fit + 256 B 对齐），把 relu(x @ w + b) 的融合 / 不融合两个计划都排一遍，再把一次执行拆成 graph compile / search / prepare / execute 四个阶段。

⏱ 45 min 难度 中 你写 mine/l4_plan_trace.py验收 ./check.sh l4参考解 solutions/l4_plan_trace.py 📖 单独讲解页

  2. 源码与资料
  3. 要用到的知识
